### Q35. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of roots of the equation <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sec</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=2</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sec</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
Multiplying by <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> ,
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/nta_abhyas/jee_main/images/d317558f-fbaa-4778-b566-842fefcfd244.png" style="width:1.79375in;height:1.16667in" />
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> at
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>6</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>6</mn></mfrac></math> in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8594;</mo></math> four times in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> for which <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi>x</mi></math> are not defined
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> is not possible
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> Number of roots in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is <math><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo></math>

</details>

### Q36. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>, then the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfenced></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>&#177;</mo><msqrt><mn>16</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>or</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
But, as <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q37. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The solution set of <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> for the inequality <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>∪</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>∪</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Case I: <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Case II: otherwise <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>&#8805;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
Hence, from case I and case II,
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8746;</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

---

### Q38. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>5</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>, where <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mi>π</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&lt;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>, then <math><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Given, <math><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mn>4</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=-</mo><mn>5</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>9</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>9</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>±</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Since, <math><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&lt;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&lt;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&lt;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&lt;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>+</mo><msqrt><mn>7</mn></msqrt></mrow><mn>9</mn></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q39. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of values of the parameter <math><mi>α</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> for which the quadratic function <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mi>x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><mfenced><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>α</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is the square of a linear function is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>For the quadratic function to be square of a linear function</p><p><math><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>&#183;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mspace linebreak="newline"/><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> or <math><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
For <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> there are two solutions for each.
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo></math> total four values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>&#945;</mi></math>.</p>

</details>

### Q40. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

The number of solutions, the equation <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi></math> has, in <math><mo>[</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>] </mo></math> is/are

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>)</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><munder><mrow><munder><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi>x</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow></munder></mrow></munder><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math> …(i)
<math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>10</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
from (i), <math><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>13</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>17</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo></math> The number of solutions <math><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q41. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of solutions of the equation <math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> in <math><mfenced open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>9</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>6</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>7</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>⇒</mo></mrow></mrow></math> <math><mn>2</mn><mo>≤</mo><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>≤</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>≤</mo><mn>16</mn></math>
Also, <math><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>≤</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>≤</mo><mn>4</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></mrow></math> so, equality is possible when <math><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mi>π</mi></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>7</mn><mi>π</mi></math>

</details>

### Q42. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>, then the value of <math><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **2**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>&#946;</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>3</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>&#946;</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
So, <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msqrt><mn>3</mn></msqrt><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>

</details>

### Q43. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of solutions of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#8704;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced open="[" close="]" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to.

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow><mi></mi></math> or <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math> (Not possible as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math> )
Hence, for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> there are <math><mn>2</mn></math> solutions in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

</details>

### Q44. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>A</mtext><mo>&#60;</mo><mtext>B</mtext><mo>&#60;</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mfrac></math> and <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></msqrt></math>, then the value of <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>.</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msqrt><mn>2</mn></msqrt></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>12</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>  
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q45. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `numerical` | *Level:* `1`

Number of solutions of <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>(</mo><mo>|</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>|</mo><mo>)</mo></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>|</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>|</mo></mrow></msup></math> in <math> <mrow> <mrow><mo>[</mo> <mrow> <mo>−</mo><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow> <mo>]</mo></mrow></mrow></math>, is equal to

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **4**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>In the interval <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> all the values of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>sin</mi><mo>|</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>|</mo></math> are positive as well as <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>|</mo><mi>cos</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>|</mo></math>
Hence in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>, equation gets reduced to, </p><p><math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></msup></math>
Taking log on both sides,
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">log</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math> , value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mi>x</mi></math> are positive in <math><msup><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>s</mi><mi>t</mi></mrow></msup></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>3</mn><mrow><mi>r</mi><mi>d</mi></mrow></msup></math> quadrant and negative in <math><msup><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi><mi>d</mi></mrow></msup></math> and <math><msup><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow><mrow><mi>t</mi><mi>h</mi></mrow></msup></math> quadrant but positive for <math><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math> in all the values of <math><mi>x</mi></math> in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mo>–</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>.</p><p>Hence, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>tan</mi><mi>x</mi></math> will repeat its value in all <math><mn>4</mn></math> quadrants, so the number of solutions are <math><mn>4</mn></math> .</p>

</details>

---

### Q46. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

General solution of the equation <math><mn>4</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>θ</mi></mrow></mrow></math> is (where, <math><mi>n</mi><mo>∈</mo><mi>Z</mi></math>)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given equation can be written as
<math><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><mi>tan</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mi>&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q47. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of ordered pairs <math><mo>(</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>,</mo><mi>y</mi><mo>)</mo></math> satisfying the equations <math><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mi> </mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi> </mi><mi>y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math> is/are

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math> <mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math> <mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** Infinite

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math> <mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

<math><mo>⇒</mo><mi> </mi><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

But, <math><mn>2</mn><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>≤</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></math>

and <math><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&gt;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>, so it has no solution

</details>

### Q48. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo></mrow></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi></mrow></mrow></math> then <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">w</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">h</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi><mo>±</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

Left hand side is greater than or equal to <math><mn>2</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8805;</mo><mn>2</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#8804;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
Hence, the given equation is <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cot</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></mrow></mrow></mrow></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mo>&#177;</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mrow></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi>n&#960;</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n&#960;</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
So, <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> and <math><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

</details>

### Q49. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

For <math><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#8804;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#8804;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> the number of points of intersection of curves <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>3</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mn>3</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>sin</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>&#177;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>8</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>8</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>4</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>8</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>8</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> and <math><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

</details>

### Q50. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The number of solutions of the equation <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> in the interval <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is equal to

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>0</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>8</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math><mn>0</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

The given equation can be written as <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
Substituting <math><mi>t</mi><mo>=</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow></math> in this equation, we get <math><msup><mrow><mi>t</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>+</mo><mi>t</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
This equation has no real roots.

</details>

### Q51. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

If <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><mrow><msup><mi>sin</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>-</mo><msup><mi>cos</mi><mn>3</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>sin</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></mrow><msqrt><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><msup><mi>cot</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi></msqrt></mfrac><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>tan</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mi>cot</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math> <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mrow><mo>∀</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced></math>, then

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>π</mi><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math><mi mathvariant="normal">θ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="}" open="{" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>Since, <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>1</mn></math></p><p>To define <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>cot</mi><mo>&#8289;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></math> and</p><p>to define <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>&#8800;</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>5</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mi>&#960;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mn>2</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></math>
Now, <math><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>-</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> or <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="|" open="|" separators="|"><mrow><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>&#8289;</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi></mrow></mrow></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi></mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> or <math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>=</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"></mi><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#952;</mi><mo>&#8712;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>,</mo><mi>&#960;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mfenced open="{" close="}" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>4</mn></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mi>&#960;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></mfenced></math></p>

</details>

### Q52. NTA Abhyas (JEE Main) 2020

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Level:* `1`

The sum of all the solutions in <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,100</mn></mrow></mfenced></math> for the equation <math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math> is

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2550</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5025</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2525</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>5050</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>5025</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">sin</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>+</mo><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">cos</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">tan</mi></mrow><mo>⁡</mo><mrow><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi></mrow></mrow><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><mi>π</mi><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>π</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn><mi>π</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>⇒</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
Given, <math><mi>x</mi><mo>∈</mo><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>0,100</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math> hence <math><mi>n</mi></math> will range from <math><mn>0</mn></math> to <math><mn>99</mn><mo>.</mo></math>
<math><mi>x</mi></math> will be <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>7</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>.</mo><mo>,</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>399</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi>S</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>+</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mi>d</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mo>=</mo><mn>50</mn><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mfrac><mrow><mn>6</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mn>99</mn><mo>×</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>502</mn><mn>5</mn></math>

</details>
